f\"duplicate feature names in dataset.features.{option_name}: \"\n            f\"{_format_names(duplicated)}\"\n        )\n\n    unknown = [name for name in names if name not in raw_columns]\n    if row-wise, treating missing values as equal.\"\"\"\n    if len(left) != len(right):\n        return False\n    left = left.reset_index(drop=True)\n    right = right.reset_index(drop=True)\n    try:\n        equal = left.eq(right) | (left.isna() & right.isna())\n        return bool(equal.all())\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return bool((left.astype(str) == right.astype(str)).all())\n\n\ndef build_feature_schema(dataset, features_config, targets=None):\n    \"\"\"Build a feature schema from the ``dataset.features`` configuration.\n\n    Selection order is: ``include`` (or the raw column order), ``exclude``,\n    constant columns (if ``drop_constant`` is set) and duplicate columns (if\n    ``drop_duplicate`` is set). The first surviving column of a group of\n    duplicate columns is kept as canonical, all later columns are recorded as\n    aliases.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(features_config, dict):\n features_config.get(\"include\"), \"include\", raw_columns, target_columns\n    )\n    exclude = _normalize_feature_option(\n        features_config.get(\"exclude\"), \"exclude\", raw_columns, target_columns\n    )\n    drop_constant = bool(features_config.get(\"drop_constant\", False))\n    drop_duplicate = bool(features_config.get(\"drop_duplicate\", False))\n\n    exclude = exclude or else:\n        selected = [column for column in include if column not in exclude]\n\n    dropped_excluded = list(exclude)\n\n    dropped_constant = []\n    if drop_constant:\n        dropped_constant              ),\n                None,\n            )\n            if canonical is None:\n                survivors.append(column)\n            else:\n                dropped_duplicate.append(column)\n                duplicate_aliases.setdefault(canonical, []).append(column)\n        selected = survivors\n\n    if not selected:\n        raise FeatureSchemaError(\n   )\n            elif isinstance(value, str):\n                # Be tolerant about the direction of a single alias mapping\n                # (canonical -> alias or alias -> canonical).\n model inputs described by a persisted feature schema.\n\n    Extra raw columns are ignored. For every canonical feature, the canonical\n    column itself or any recorded alias may be provided. If more than one\n    source is provided, the values must agree row-wise.\n    \"\"\"\n    input_features = list(schema.get(\"input_features\") or [])\n    if not input_features:\n        raise        self.feature_schema = self._load_persisted_feature_schema(dic)\n        getattr(self, self.command)()\n\n    def _create_model(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        fetch a model depending on the provided type and algorithm by the user and return it\n        @return: class of the chosen model\n        \"\"\"\n        model_type: str = self.model_props.get(\"type\")\n        model_algorithm: str = self.model_props.get(\"algorithm\")\n        use_cv = self.model_props.get(\"use_cv_estimator\", None)\n\n        model_args = None\n        if not model_type or not model_algorithm:\n            raise Exception(f\"model_type and algorithm cannot be None\")\n        algorithms: dict = models_dict.get(\n            model_type\n        )  # extract all algorithms as a dictionary\n        model = algorithms.get(\n            model_algorithm\n        )  # extract model class depending on the algorithm\n        logger.info(\n            f\"Solving a {model_type} problem using ===> {model_algorithm}\"\n        )\n        if not model:\n            raise Exception(\"Model not found in the algorithms list\")\n        else:\n            model_props_args = self.model_props.get(\"arguments\", None)\n            if model_props_args and type(model_props_args) == dict:\n                model_args = model_props_args\n            elif not model_props_args or model_props_args.lower() == \"default\":\n                model_args = None\n\n            if use_cv:\n                model_class = model.get(\"cv_class\", None)\n                if model_class:\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"cross validation estimator detected. \"\n                        f\"Switch to the CV version of the {model_algorithm} algorithm\"\n                    )\n                else:\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"No CV class found for the {model_algorithm} algorithm\"\n                    )\n            else:\n                model_class = model.get(\"class\")\n            logger.info(\n                f\"model arguments: \\n\" f\"{self.model_props.get('arguments')}\"\n            )\n            model = (\n                model_class(**kwargs)\n                if not model_args\n                else model_class(**model_args)\n            )\n            return model, model_args\n\n    def _save_model(self, model):\n        \"\"\"\n        save the model to a binary file\n        @param model: model to save\n        @return: bool\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            if not os.path.exists(self.results_path):\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"creating model_results folder to save results...\\n\"\n                    f\"path of the results folder: {self.results_path}\"\n                )\n                os.mkdir(self.results_path)\n            else:\n                logger.info(f\"Folder {self.results_path} already exists\")\n                logger.warning(\n                    f\"data in the {self.results_path} folder will be overridden. If you don't \"\n                    f\"want this, then move the current {self.results_path} to another path\"\n                )\n\n        except OSError:\n            logger.exception(\n                f\"Creating the directory {self.results_path} failed \"\n            )\n        else:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"Successfully created the directory in {self.results_path} \"\n            )\n            joblib.dump(model, open(self.default_model_path, \"wb\"))\n            return True\n\n    def _load_model(self, f: str = \"\"):\n        \"\"\"\n        load a saved model from file\n        @param f: path to model\n        @return: loaded model\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            if not f:\n                logger.info(f\"result path: {self.results_path} \")\n                logger.info(f\"loading model form {self.default_model_path} \")\n                model = joblib.load(open(self.default_model_path, \"rb\"))\n            else:\n                logger.info(f\"loading from {f}\")\n                model = joblib.load(open(f, \"rb\"))\n            return model\n        except FileNotFoundError:\n            logger.error(f\"File not found in {self.default_model_path} \")\n\n    def _load_persisted_feature_schema(self, description):\n        \"\"\"\n        load the feature schema that was persisted while fitting the model\n        @param description: parsed description.json of the trained model\n        @return: feature schema as a dict or None\n        \"\"\"\n        candidates = []\n        recorded_path = description.get(\"feature_schema_path\")\n        if recorded_path:\n            # prefer the recorded path, but fall back to the description\n            # directory in case the model results were moved/copied\n            candidates.append(Path(str(recorded_path)))\n            try:\n                candidates.append(\n                    Path(str(self.description_file)).parent\n                    / Constants.feature_schema_file\n                )\n            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                pass\n\n        for candidate in candidates:\n            try:\n                if candidate and candidate.exists():\n                    schema = load_feature_schema(candidate)\n                    if isinstance(schema, dict):\n                        self.feature_schema_path = str(candidate)\n                        logger.info(\n                            f\"loaded feature schema from {candidate}\"\n                        )\n                        return schema\n            except Exception as ex:\n                logger.warning(\n                    f\"could not load feature schema from {candidate}: {ex}\"\n                )\n\n        schema = schema_from_description(description)\n        if schema is not None:\n            logger.info(\"rebuilt feature schema from the description file\")\n        return schema\n\n    def _save_feature_schema(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        persist the feature schema of the trained model in the results folder\n        @return: path of the persisted schema or None\n        \"\"\"\n        schema = getattr(self, \"feature_schema\", None)\n        if schema is None:\n            return None\n\n        schema_path = (\n            Path(str(self.results_path)) / Constants.feature_schema_file\n        )\n        try:\n            saved_path = save_feature_schema(schema, schema_path)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            raise FeatureSchemaError(\n                f\"error while persisting the feature schema: {ex}\"\n            )\n        self.feature_schema_path = str(saved_path)\n        logger.info(\n            f\"feature schema saved successfully and can be found in {saved_path}\"\n        )\n        return self.feature_schema_path\n\n    def _apply_feature_selection(self, dataset, attributes, target):\n        \"\"\"\n        build (fit) or apply (evaluate, predict) the persisted feature schema\n        @param dataset: raw dataset as a pandas dataframe\n        @param attributes: list of the current dataset columns\n        @param target: the data preparation target (fit, evaluate, predict ...)\n        @return: tuple of the selected dataset and its columns\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.command == \"fit\" and target in (\"fit\", \"fit_cluster\"):\n            features_config = self.dataset_props.get(\"features\", None)\n            if features_config is not None:\n                targets = (\n                    []\n                    if self.model_type == \"clustering\"\n                    else list(self.target or [])\n                )\n                self.feature_schema = build_feature_schema(\n                    dataset=dataset,\n                    features_config=features_config,\n                    targets=targets,\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"selected model input features: \"\n                    f\"{self.feature_schema.get('input_features')}\"\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"dropped features: \"\n                    f\"{self.feature_schema.get('dropped_features')}\"\n                )\n\n        schema = getattr(self, \"feature_schema\", None)\n        if not schema:\n            return dataset, attributes\n\n        feature_frame = apply_feature_schema(dataset, schema)\n        if target in (\"predict\", \"fit_cluster\"):\n            return feature_frame, list(feature_frame.columns)\n\n        targets = (\n            [] if self.model_type == \"clustering\" else list(self.target or [])\n        )\n        missing_targets = [\n            item for item in targets if item not in dataset.columns\n        ]\n        if missing_targets:\n            raise FeatureSchemaError(\n                \"chosen target(s) to predict must exist in the dataset: \"\n                f\"{missing_targets}\"\n            )\n        if not targets:\n            return feature_frame, list(feature_frame.columns)\n\n        target_frame = dataset[targets].reset_index(drop=True)\n        feature_frame = feature_frame.reset_index(drop=True)\n        combined = pd.concat([feature_frame, target_frame], axis=1)\n        return combined, list(combined.columns)\n\n    def _prepare_fit_data(self):\n        return self._process_data(target=\"fit\")\n\n    def _prepare_eval_data(self):\n        return self._process_data(target=\"evaluate\")\n\n    def _process_data(self, target=\"fit\"):\n        \"\"\"\n        read and return data as x and y\n        @return: list of separate x and y\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n            assert isinstance(\n                self.target, list\n            ), \"provide target(s) as a list in the yaml file\"\n            assert (\n                len(self.target) > 0\n            ), \"please provide at least a target to predict\"\n\n        try:\n            read_data_options = self.dataset_props.get(\"read_data_options\", {})\n            dataset = read_data_to_df(\n                data_path=self.data_path, **read_data_options\n            )\n            logger.info(f\"dataset shape: {dataset.shape}\")\n            attributes = list(dataset.columns)\n            logger.info(f\"dataset attributes: {attributes}\")\n\n            # select the raw features according to the feature schema\n            dataset, attributes = self._apply_feature_selection(\n                dataset=dataset, attributes=attributes, target=target\n            )\n            logger.info(f\"dataset attributes after feature selection: {attributes}\")\n\n            # handle missing values in the dataset\n            preprocess_props = self.dataset_props.get(\"preprocess\", None)\n            if preprocess_props:\n                # handle encoding\n                encoding = preprocess_props.get(\"encoding\")\n                if encoding:\n                    encoding_type = encoding.get(\"type\", None)\n                    column = encoding.get(\"column\", None)\n                    if column in attributes:\n                        dataset, classes_map = encode(\n                            df=dataset,\n                            encoding_type=encoding_type.lower(),\n                            column=column,\n                        )\n                        if classes_map:\n                            self.dataset_props[\n                                \"label_encoding_classes\"\n                            ] = classes_map\n                            logger.info(\n                                f\"adding classes_map to dataset props: \\n{classes_map}\"\n                            )\n                        logger.info(\n                            f\"shape of the dataset after encoding => {dataset.shape}\"\n                        )\n\n                # preprocessing strategy: mean, median, mode etc..\n                strategy = preprocess_props.get(\"missing_values\")\n                if strategy:\n                    dataset = handle_missing_values(dataset, strategy=strategy)\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"shape of the dataset after handling missing values => {dataset.shape}\"\n                    )\n\n            if target == \"predict\" or target == \"fit_cluster\":\n                x = _reshape(dataset.to_numpy())\n                if not preprocess_props:\n                    return x\n                scaling_props = preprocess_props.get(\"scale\", None)\n                if not scaling_props:\n                    return x\n                else:\n                    scaling_method = scaling_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                    return normalize(x, method=scaling_method)\n\n            if any(col not in attributes for col in self.target):\n                raise Exception(\n                    \"chosen target(s) to predict must exist in the dataset\"\n                )\n\n            y = pd.concat([dataset.pop(x) for x in self.target], axis=1)\n            x = _reshape(dataset.to_numpy())\n            y = _reshape(y.to_numpy())\n            logger.info(f\"y shape: {y.shape} and x shape: {x.shape}\")\n\n            # handle data scaling\n            if preprocess_props:\n                scaling_props = preprocess_props.get(\"scale\", None)\n                if scaling_props:\n                    scaling_method = scaling_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                    scaling_target = scaling_props.get(\"target\", None)\n                    if scaling_target == \"all\":\n                        x = normalize(x, method=scaling_method)\n                        y = normalize(y, method=scaling_method)\n                    elif scaling_target == \"inputs\":\n                        x = normalize(x, method=scaling_method)\n                    elif scaling_target == \"outputs\":\n                        y = normalize(y, method=scaling_method)\n\n            if target == \"evaluate\":\n                return x, y\n\n            split_options = self.dataset_props.get(\"split\", None)\n            if not split_options:\n                return x, y, None, None\n            test_size = split_options.get(\"test_size\")\n            shuffle = split_options.get(\"shuffle\")\n            stratify = split_options.get(\"stratify\")\n            x_train, x_test, y_train, y_test = train_test_split(\n                x,\n                y,\n                test_size=test_size,\n                shuffle=shuffle,\n                stratify=None\n                if not stratify or stratify.lower() == \"default\"\n                else stratify,\n            )\n\n            return x_train, y_train, x_test, y_test\n\n        except FeatureSchemaError:\n            raise\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"error occured while preparing the data: {e}\")\n\n    def _prepare_clustering_data(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        preprocess data for the clustering algorithm\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._process_data(target=\"fit_cluster\")\n\n    def _prepare_predict_data(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        preprocess predict data to get similar data to the one used when training the model\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._process_data(target=\"predict\")\n\n    def get_evaluation(self, model, x_test, y_true, y_pred, **kwargs):\n        try:\n            res = evaluate_model(\n                model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=False,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(e)\n            res = evaluate_model(\n                model_type=self.model_type,\n                model=model,\n                x_test=x_test,\n                y_pred=y_pred,\n                y_true=y_true,\n                get_score_only=True,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n        return res\n\n    def fit(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        fit a machine learning model and save it to a file along with a description.json file\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        x_train = None\n        x_test = None\n        y_train = None\n        y_test = None\n        cv_results = None\n        eval_results = None\n        cv_params = None\n        hp_search_results = {}\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            x_train = self._prepare_clustering_data()\n        else:\n            x_train, y_train, x_test, y_test = self._prepare_fit_data()\n        self.model, model_args = self._create_model(**kwargs)\n        logger.info(f\"executing a {self.model.__class__.__name__} algorithm...\")\n\n        # convert to multioutput if there is more than one target to predict:\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\" and len(self.target) > 1:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"predicting multiple targets detected. Hence, the model will be automatically \"\n                f\"converted to a multioutput model\"\n            )\n            self.model = (\n                MultiOutputClassifier(self.model)\n                if self.model_type == \"classification\"\n                else MultiOutputRegressor(self.model)\n            )\n\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n            cv_params = self.model_props.get(\"cross_validate\", None)\n            if not cv_params:\n                logger.info(f\"cross validation is not provided\")\n            else:\n                # perform cross validation\n                logger.info(\"performing cross validation ...\")\n                cv_results = cross_validate(\n                    estimator=self.model, X=x_train, y=y_train, **cv_params\n                )\n            hyperparams_props = self.model_props.get(\n                \"hyperparameter_search\", None\n            )\n            if hyperparams_props:\n\n                # perform hyperparameter search\n                method = hyperparams_props.get(\"method\", None)\n                grid_params = hyperparams_props.get(\"parameter_grid\", None)\n                hp_args = hyperparams_props.get(\"arguments\", None)\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"Performing hyperparameter search using -> {method}\"\n                )\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"Grid parameters entered by the user: {grid_params}\"\n                )\n                logger.info(f\"Additional hyperparameter arguments: {hp_args}\")\n                best_estimator, best_params, best_score = hyperparameter_search(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    method=method,\n                    params=grid_params,\n                    x_train=x_train,\n                    y_train=y_train,\n                    **hp_args,\n                )\n                hp_search_results[\"best_params\"] = best_params\n                hp_search_results[\"best_score\"] = best_score\n                self.model = best_estimator\n\n            self.model.fit(x_train, y_train)\n\n        else:  # if the model type is clustering\n            self.model.fit(x_train)\n\n        saved = self._save_model(self.model)\n        if saved:\n            logger.info(\n                f\"model saved successfully and can be found in the {self.results_path} folder\"\n            )\n\n        # persist the selected raw feature schema along with the model\n        self._save_feature_schema()\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            eval_results = self.model.score(x_train)\n        else:\n            if x_test is None:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"no split options was provided. training score will be calculated\"\n                )\n                eval_results = self.model.score(x_train, y_train)\n\n            else:\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"split option detected. The performance will be automatically evaluated \"\n                    f\"using the test data portion\"\n                )\n                y_pred = self.model.predict(x_test)\n                eval_results = self.get_evaluation(\n                    model=self.model,\n                    x_test=x_test,\n                    y_true=y_test,\n                    y_pred=y_pred,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n\n        fit_description = {\n            \"model\": self.model.__class__.__name__,\n            \"arguments\": model_args if model_args else \"default\",\n            \"type\": self.model_props[\"type\"],\n            \"algorithm\": self.model_props[\"algorithm\"],\n            \"dataset_props\": self.dataset_props,\n            \"model_props\": self.model_props,\n            \"data_path\": self.data_path,\n            \"train_data_shape\": x_train.shape,\n            \"test_data_shape\": None if x_test is None else x_test.shape,\n            \"train_data_size\": x_train.shape[0],\n            \"test_data_size\": None if x_test is None else x_test.shape[0],\n            \"results_path\": str(self.results_path),\n            \"model_path\": str(self.default_model_path),\n            \"target\": None if self.model_type == \"clustering\" else self.target,\n            \"results_on_test_data\": eval_results,\n            \"hyperparameter_search_results\": hp_search_results,\n        }\n\n        # record the selected raw feature schema, if one was configured\n        if self.feature_schema is not None:\n            dropped_features = self.feature_schema.get(\n                \"dropped_features\", {}\n            )\n            fit_description[\"feature_schema_path\"] = self.feature_schema_path\n            fit_description[\"input_features\"] = list(\n                self.feature_schema.get(\"input_features\", [])\n            )\n            fit_description[\"dropped_features\"] = {\n                \"excluded\": list(dropped_features.get(\"excluded\", [])),\n                \"constant\": list(dropped_features.get(\"constant\", [])),\n                \"duplicate\": list(dropped_features.get(\"duplicate\", [])),\n            }\n            fit_description[\"duplicate_feature_aliases\"] = {\n                canonical: list(aliases)\n                for canonical, aliases in self.feature_schema.get(\n                    \"duplicate_feature_aliases\", {}\n                ).items()\n            }\n\n        if self.model_type == \"clustering\":\n            clustering_res = {\n                \"cluster_centers\": self.model.cluster_centers_.tolist(),\n                \"cluster_labels\": self.model.labels_.tolist(),\n            }\n            fit_description[\"clustering_results\"] = clustering_res\n\n        if cv_params:\n            cv_res = {\n                \"fit_time\": cv_results[\"fit_time\"].tolist(),\n                \"score_time\": cv_results[\"score_time\"].tolist(),\n                \"test_score\": cv_results[\"test_score\"].tolist(),\n            }\n            fit_description[\"cross_validation_params\"] = cv_params\n            fit_description[\"cross_validation_results\"] = cv_res\n\n        try:\n            logger.info(f\"saving fit description to {self.description_file}\")\n            with open(self.description_file, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                json.dump(fit_description, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(\n                f\"Error while storing the fit description file: {e}\"\n            )\n\n    def evaluate(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        evaluate a pre-fitted model and save results to a evaluation.json\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        x_val = None\n        y_true = None\n        eval_results = None\n\n        try:\n            model = self._load_model()\n            if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n                x_val, y_true = self._prepare_eval_data()\n                y_pred = model.predict(x_val)\n                eval_results = self.get_evaluation(\n                    model=model,\n                    x_test=x_val,\n                    y_true=y_true,\n                    y_pred=y_pred,\n                    **kwargs,\n                )\n            else:\n                x_val = self._prepare_clustering_data()\n                y_pred = model.predict(x_val)\n                eval_results = model.score(x_val, y_pred)\n\n            logger.info(f\"saving fit description to {self.evaluation_file}\")\n            with open(self.evaluation_file, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                json.dump(eval_results, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)\n\n        except FeatureSchemaError:\n            raise\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"error occured during evaluation: {e}\")\n\n    def _get_predictions(self, **kwargs):\n        \"\"\"\n        use a pre-fitted model to generate predictions\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n            model = self._load_model(f=self.model_path)\n            x_val = (\n                self._prepare_predict_data()\n            )  # the same is used for clustering\n            y_pred = model.predict(x_val)\n            y_pred = _reshape(y_pred)\n            logger.info(\n                f\"predictions shape: {y_pred.shape} | shape len: {len(y_pred.shape)}\"\n            )\n            logger.info(f\"predict on targets: {self.target}\")\n            if not self.target:\n                self.target = [\"result\"]\n            df_pred = pd.DataFrame.from_dict(\n                {\n                    self.target[i]: y_pred[:, i]\n                    if len(y_pred.shape) > 1\n                    else y_pred\n                    for i in range(len(self.target))\n                }\n            )\n            return df_pred\n\n        except FeatureSchemaError:\n            raise\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"Error while preparing predictions: {e}\")\n\n    def predict(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        generate predictions and save them as csv. This is used as a command from cli\n        \"\"\"\n\n        df_pred = self._get_predictions()\n        self.predictions = df_pred\n        logger.info(f\"saving the predictions to {self.prediction_file}\")\n        df_pred.to_csv(self.prediction_file, index=False)\n\n    def _get_export_input_width(self, default: int = 4) -> int:\n        \"\"\"\n        derive the number of model inputs from the fit description file\n        @param default: fallback width if the description cannot be used\n        @return: expected model input width\n        \"\"\"\n        description = None\n        try:\n            with open(self.description_file) as f:\n                description = json.load(f)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            logger.warning(\n                f\"could not read the description file to derive the model \"\n                f\"input width: {ex}\"\n            )\n            return default\n\n        input_features = description.get(\"input_features\")\n        if input_features:\n            return len(input_features)\n\n        train_data_shape = description.get(\"train_data_shape\")\n        if (\n            isinstance(train_data_shape, (list, tuple))\n            and len(train_data_shape) >= 2\n        ):\n            try:\n                return int(train_data_shape[1])\n            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                pass\n        return default\n\n    def export(self):\n        \"\"\"\n        export a sklearn model to ONNX. This is used as a command from cli\n        @return: None\n        \"\"\"\n        try:\n\n            logger.info(\n                f\"Trying to load sklearn model from directory - {self.model_path} \"\n            )\n            model = self._load_model(f=self.model_path)\n            input_width = self._get_export_input_width()\n            logger.info(\n                f\"exporting the model with an input width of {input_width}\"\n            )\n            initial_type = [\n                (\"float_input\", FloatTensorType([None, input_width]))\n            ]\n            onx = convert_sklearn(model, initial_types=initial_type)\n            \n            # check if model_results folder is present and create if absent\n            if not os.path.exists(self.results_path):\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"creating model_results folder to save results...\\n\"\n                    f\"path of the results folder: {self.results_path}\"\n                )\n                os.mkdir(self.results_path)\n            else:\n                logger.info(f\"Folder {self.results_path} already exists\")\n                logger.warning(\n                    f\"data in the {self.results_path} folder will be overridden. If you don't \"\n                    f\"want this, then move the current {self.results_path} to another path\"\n                )\n            \n            with open(self.default_onnx_model_path, \"wb\") as f:\n                f.write(onx.SerializeToString())\n            logger.info(\n                f\"Successfully saved exported onnx model at - {self.default_onnx_model_path} \"\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.exception(f\"Error while exporting model: {e}\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def create_init_mock_file(\n        model_type=None, model_name=None, target=None, *args, **kwargs\n    ):\n        path = configs.get(\"init_file_path\", None)\n        if not path:\n            raise Exception(\"You need to provide a path for the init file\")\n\n        dataset_props = Igel.default_dataset_props\n        model_props = Igel.default_model_props\n        if model_type:\n            logger.info(f\"user selected model type = {model_type}\")\n            model_props[\"type\"] = model_type\n        if model_name:\n            logger.info(f\"user selected algorithm = {model_name}\")\n            model_props[\"algorithm\"] = model_name\n\n        logger.info(f\"initalizing a default igel.yaml in {path}\")\n        default_data = {\n            \"dataset\": dataset_props,\n            \"model\": model_props,\n            \"target\": [\"provide "/app/igel/feature_schema.py", "content": "\"\"\"Feature schema handling.\n\nA feature schema describes the raw columns that have been selected as model\ninputs while fitting. It is persisted next to the trained model and applied\nagain whenever the model is evaluated, used for predictions or served through\nthe REST API.\n\nThe schema is a plain dictionary so that it can be stored with ``joblib`` and\nembedded (partially) in ``description.json``::\n\n    {\n        \"input_features\": [\"a\", \"b\"],\n        \"dropped_features\": {\n            \"excluded\": [],\n            \"constant\": [\"c\"],\n            \"duplicate\": [\"b_copy\"],\n        },\n        \"duplicate_feature_aliases\": {\"b\": [\"b_copy\"]},\n    }\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport joblib\nimport pandas as pd\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass FeatureSchemaError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a feature schema is invalid or cannot be applied.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _format_names(names):\n    \"\"\"Return a comma separated representation of feature names.\"\"\"\n    return \", \".join(repr(name) for name in names)\n\n\ndef _normalize_feature_option(\n    value, option_name, raw_columns, target_columns\n):\n    \"\"\"Validate and normalize an ``include`` or ``exclude`` option.\n\n    The option may be a single column name or a list of unique, non-empty raw\n    feature names. Target columns are not allowed in either option.\n    \"\"\"\n    if value is None:\n        return None\n\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        names = [value]\n    elif isinstance(value, (list, row-wise, treating missing values as equal.\"\"\"\n    if len(left) != len(right):\n        return False\n    left = left.reset_index(drop=True)\n    right = right.reset_index(drop=True)\n    try:\n        equal = left.eq(right) | (left.isna() & right.isna())\n        return bool(equal.all())\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return bool((left.astype(str) == right.astype(str)).all())\n\n\ndef build_feature_schema(dataset, features_config, targets=None):\n    \"\"\"Build a feature schema from the ``dataset.features`` configuration.\n\n    Selection order is: ``include`` (or the raw column order), ``exclude``,\n    constant columns (if ``drop_constant`` is set) and duplicate columns (if\n    ``drop_duplicate`` is set). The first surviving column of a group of\n    duplicate columns is kept as canonical, all later columns are recorded as\n    aliases.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not isinstance(features_config, dict):\n \"\"\"\n\n        if self.model_type != \"clustering\":\n            assert isinstance(\n                self.target, list\n            ), \"provide target(s) as a list in the yaml file\"\n
